## 2026-11-19 04:56 Europe/Berlin — C.2 pencil gate removed

C.2e supplies a complete all-characteristic proof of extension-stable universal A-prime_hom=>A_hom, with a lexicographic induction on codimension and dimension. The exceptional blow-up contribution is handled by the lower-codimension induction; the generic cycle is transferred from a finite extension of k(t) before taking its closure. Both cycles and frobenius independently checked the proof.

C.2f proves the converse by restriction and the projection formula. C.2g gives the exact critical-dimension reduction n=2i+1 using a hyperplane in X×P^d whose restriction over X−Y is a coordinate hyperplane. Its zeroth Chow coefficient vanishes before passing to cycle equivalence, so Chow localization is used legitimately. This latter reduction also works modulo algebraic equivalence, without claiming the homological pencil argument transfers automatically.

The remaining A-double-prime_hom=>A-prime_hom gate is now exactly universal standard conjecture A (equivalently the universal inverse-Lefschetz assertion with product quantifiers). Affine vanishing alone does not settle it. The positivity-only implication and complete conditional Weil estimates remain separate unfinished parts of C.2.

## 2026-11-19 06:22 Europe/Berlin — local degree-one and integral family gates refined

This is an incremental entry, not the first scheduled movement review.

- C.3i refutes unrestricted strict local Picard injectivity and isomorphism in every proposed degree-one dimension range: the explicit normal complex cone has surjective restriction with kernel(C,+)^3. C.3j proves that the same kernel survives quotienting by ordinary algebraic equivalence, whereas the ordinary J1 restriction in the example is an isomorphism. This removes the earlier ambiguity between local Picard identity components and ordinary Chow algebraic equivalence.
- C.3k proves prime-to-residue-characteristic torsion Picard injectivity for excellent normal strictly henselian rings in every dimensionN≥3 with regular punctured divisor, including mixed characteristic. Using the separate algebraic-family compactification argument and a closed subgroup of an abelian variety, it also proves ordinary J1 injectivity for algebraizable normal characteristic-zero germs already in dimension3. Surjectivity and the nonalgebraizable ordinary-J1 assertion remain separate gates.
- C.3l proves that the pair of universal abelian injectivity assertions is equivalent to ordinary Beauville negative-index vanishing for all abelian varieties. The proof uses a parameter Jacobian and shifts the grading by one. This unifies the two criteria but does not prove their vanishing or improve the fixed-dimension test cases; it must not be counted as an independent status change in those cases.
- C.4r narrows the residual dimension2/3 splitting problem in every algebraically closed characteristic to one integral numerical surface class on A×dual(A): P²/2 orP⁴/24. The already proved small-integer factorizations make this condition sufficient, and all-prime cohomological representatives imply numerical integral membership. C.4s settles every supersingular surface via a checked principal-polarization theorem. General residual2/3-prime cases remain.
- E.3m–n show that the rational and integral whole-family converses overC are equivalent, and both are equivalent to N=ker(Walker). The countability argument handles torsion-valued family maps without assuming a uniform exponent. The general equality itself is still unproved; the false integral ordinary-Abel–Jacobi equality is not reintroduced.

# Scheduled mathematical review 6: comparison with review 5

The target time is 19 November 2026, 10:50 UTC. The recording helper supplies the actual completion time and rejects an early or duplicate review. We compare with the completed review at 09:51:21.318415 UTC.

## Qualifying movement: YES

The main C.3 gate has strictly decreased in two precise respects.

1. **The ordinary complex local restriction classification is complete.** At review 5, higher-degree injectivity remained open for r>=2 and N>=2r+1, with first test (N,r)=(5,2). The fully proved and independently audited C.3ao provides infinite-order ordinary algebraically trivial kernel classes throughout that range. Its proof uses an actual Picard kernel, actual fixed-product curve families, an absolute differential detector, and the full exceptional-boundary recursion. C.3ap combines this with the previously certified lower-dimensional kernels and the source-zero cases. For r>=2, universal injectivity holds exactly for N<=r, universal surjectivity exactly for N<=r+1, and universal isomorphism exactly for N<=r, integrally and rationally. For r=1, the independently proved table is injectivity exactly for N=1 or N>=3, surjectivity exactly for N<=2, and isomorphism exactly for N=1. The original C.3ao--ap audit completed at 10:01:26.541461 UTC, after review 5. The later reconstruction audit is not counted again.

2. **The integral positive-characteristic top-finiteness branch is false in every nontrivial dimension.** C.3an and C.3aq first gave the dimension-three constructions. The newly completed C.3as gives, for every algebraically closed k of characteristic p>0 and every N>=3, an explicitly specified normal excellent strict-local cone with regular puncture X and an ordinary integral quotient B^(N-1)(X) surjecting onto (k,+). Root and the cycles branch read and checked the complete all-dimensional proof; exact primary inputs and the supplementary 25-case tower and 11-case sign certificate are retained. The coefficient-one Milnor-symbol residue works in all characteristics. Its target has exponent p, so the rational and characteristic-zero top questions are left open.

These are actual mathematical resolutions of previously open parts of C.3, not restatements of the same gate. The conclusion is YES and the consecutive dry-review count resets to zero.

## Work not counted as movement

C.3ar is a restricted positive top-quotient theorem for products of curves with the specified external-product polarization and Qbar model; it leaves the unrestricted rational top gate unchanged. The semihomogeneous Chern-construction parity obstruction and the genus-five/correlated-monodromy calculations in C.4 exclude methods or establish necessary conditions but do not settle the remaining generic type-(1,2) case. The quintic-surface cone analysis has not yet determined the ordinary-family image. Fresh source openings, finite reruns, typesetting, registering results, and restoration after workspace loss do not count as movement.

No other main open row has changed status since review 5. The complete 41-row task-and-Lead snapshot is appended by the recording helper.

## Remaining main gates

- C.1--C.2: the exact universal positivity/shifted Griffiths and standard-conjecture gates, including the arbitrary-source image quantifier; the first unresolved characteristic-zero standard-D case in the stated dimension audit is codimension three on a fivefold.
- C.3: universal global projective Griffiths/Beauville injectivity, unrestricted rational top quotient B^(N-1) for N>=3 (including the complex case), and higher-degree positive-characteristic ordinary restriction. The coefficient-field and p-primary qualifications in the proved degree-one positive-characteristic table remain unchanged.
- C.4: integral algebraicity of the specified Poincare divided powers for generic End=Z type-(1,2) surfaces and general nonprincipal threefolds, and arbitrary supersingular threefolds in characteristics two and three.
- D.4/E.2: all-motives integral-spectrum effectivity retains the exact Tate-surjectivity gate in the stated motivic framework; common geometric polarization beyond the proved abelian-family scope remains.
- A.8/Lead A3 and E.5/Lead E3: the incomplete original phrase at scan 15:70 and the unspecified historical meaning of continuous H1 remain exact textual limitations, not invented mathematical assertions.

1. **Nonsurjectivity throughout the lower and middle range.** C.3at and its dimension extension C.3av prove, in every positive characteristic, failure of ordinary rational restriction surjectivity for every r>=1 and r+2<=N<=2r+1, over the specified transcendental algebraically closed fields. The source puncture is regular with zero positive Chow group; the target contains an actual ordinary infinite-order class. The argument proves the all-prime Hasse/Witt input, the generic-point support obstruction, the integral join/suspension correspondence and the slope exclusion of the hyperplane image. The r>=2 range was part of the remaining characteristic-p restriction gate at review6. The previously established degree-one qualifications are not counted a second time.
2. **Noninjectivity through the critical dimension.** C.3aw proves failure for r>=2 and r+1<=N<=2r−1. C.3ay closes N=2r. Its explicit elliptic incidence has a reduced base of exactly3r points and a regular quotient-bundle section. The integral Chern annihilator specializes to r a^(r−1)lambda; the exact source quotient is Pic(E)/Z(M^r), where the generic infinite-order lambda survives. Thus the source class is infinite-order and restricts to zero integrally even when p divides r. The strict-local construction, actual ordinary family and all characteristic-two/three checks are part of the complete proof. Together these results remove r+1<=N<=2r from the remaining positive-characteristic injectivity gate.

The higher ordinary restriction intervals still needing resolution for r>=2 are injectivity at N>=2r+1 and surjectivity at N>=2r+2, with the existing field and coefficient distinctions retained. This is a genuine reduction of the gate recorded at review6. The consecutive dry-review count therefore resets to zero.

4. The positive-characteristic higher-restriction investigations identify precise method failures. In particular, a p-primary infinitesimal K-class multiplied by an ordinary p-divisible family class is zero, so the complex nilpotent construction cannot simply be transported to characteristic p. Formal cohomological cycle lifting does not supply the required actual ordinary families. These are obstructions to methods, not proofs or counterexamples for the remaining ranges.

## Main-gate assessment

**Qualifying movement: YES. Consecutive dry reviews: zero.** C.3bf gives an actual counterexample to the remaining unrestricted integral ordinary-divisor injectivity assertion: over k=Fpbar with p=5, for every local dimension N≥4, an explicitly specified excellent normal strictly henselian local ring with regular puncture X and regular punctured principal divisor Y has an injected (Z/5)^3 in the kernel of Pic(X)_alg→Pic(Y)_alg. The family is an actual family on the fixed product X×P. This changes a main C.3 injectivity subquestion from an unresolved possible p-primary kernel to a proved nonzero integral kernel. It is not merely a reformulation, a field-restricted positive theorem, or a source acquisition.

The theorem does not assert a rational failure and does not change any higher-codimension range. Its literal characteristic-five scope remains explicit. The source and target geometry, first-neighborhood Picard extension, rank-three Kodaira–Spencer calculation, ordinary Verschiebung subgroup, and full proper-curve/GAGA/Popescu construction were independently checked by both peers and the root. The revised complete proof was saved at13:49:26; the peers' full revised reads were completed at13:49:28 and13:50:16. The exact finite program began at13:51:48.836254 and passed41checks in0.07533210799738299seconds. Exact primary Ext and ordinary-Verschiebung inputs were opened during the scheduled review; those are validation of the existing proof, not a new approach used to prolong the interval.

The repaired proof avoids an unnecessary global local-Picard-functor claim. It realizes each kernel point through a fixed proper curve, retains the complete unit-band obstruction or its equivalent direct Poincare lifting calculation, lifts through every later thickening, and uses henselian smooth approximation preserving the entire first jet. Endpoint uniqueness and separate completion detection prove both source nonvanishing and target vanishing. No universal bundle over the whole abelian variety is assumed to algebraize.

Review8 was NO with a dry streak of one. This genuine integral counterexample resets the streak. Research therefore continues; the next scheduled review remains14:50UTC and the next scheduled45-minute checkpoint is14:05UTC. The mathematical programme is still INCOMPLETE.

The following other work was completed or further checked in this window and is not itself counted as movement:

1. **C.3be was strengthened within its residue-field-restricted branch.** The final theorem proves rational one-cycle Chow vanishing for every excellent local domain of dimension at least two whose residue field is the algebraic closure of a finite field, including mixed characteristic. Finite semilocal normalization removes henselianity; ambient normality and a regular puncture are not required. The entire proof and the exact source inputs were independently checked. It does not settle the unrestricted arbitrary-field top ordinary quotient, so it is conservatively not counted as movement of that main gate.
2. **The characteristic-zero actual-family gap was examined further.** For a fixed bundle on E×C, relative Serre vanishing eventually removes its deformation obstruction, but no uniform fixed-representative argument was proved for the desired K0 classes. The neighboring Ballico–Gasparim–Köppe theorem concerns a curve exceptional locus or an already existing formal bundle. It does not supply the missing family. A complete auxiliary constant-curve K2 argument shows that the explicit first jet with differential d(epsilon) wedge dx/y cannot algebraize over the algebraic henselization. The required transfer from a top K0 family to that K2 group remains unproved. Pencil base change, the specified ample product-of-curves cover, surface normalization, and numerical quotients do not supply the missing ordinary top relation.
3. **The higher positive-characteristic restriction ranges did not change.** Finite pro-descent gives a precise supported-perfect-class description of the restriction kernel, but it does not identify that kernel with the needed ordinary cycle families. The characteristic-zero product construction vanishes integrally when a p-primary factor is multiplied by a p-divisible ordinary family factor. The established incidence detector has a dimension ceiling N≤2r. C.3bf now supplies an actual degree-one kernel, but a nonzero higher product with a suitable nondivisible factor is still unproved: the nonsplit-ribbon connecting map can kill the first differential detector.
4. **C.4ab remains an equivalent integral criterion.** The generic End=Z type-(1,2) abelian-surface case still lacks either the required odd integral correspondence or a proof excluding every such correspondence. The opened Schoen, Gaussian Weil-cycle, and 2026 hyperkähler constructions retain the recorded integral-order, parity, or isogeny-normalization obstacles. They do not exclude arbitrary cycles. The proposed integral-Tate shortcut requires Tate for divisors on all smooth projective surfaces, beyond the abelian scope of the source being used. Conditional higher-genus lattice calculations have not been promoted to geometric theorems.
5. **The Patel–Ravindra source diagnostic is narrowly scoped.** The explicit P4/quadric example refutes the literal open-complement Lemma 3.2 in the actually read versions. It is not a refutation of the paper's main torsion theorem, and no numbered result in this package relies on that lemma. Independent source readings and its exact finite certificate are retained. This source rejection does not move a main task.

## Work completed and why it is not counted as movement

The fixed-parameter obstruction has been calculated exactly in ordinary nonconnective perfect K-theory after inverting p. For a proper regular modification Z of Spec(R) with reduced exceptional fibre E and a fixed smooth proper parameter P, the cokernel of K0(Z×P)→K0(E×P) is K−1(Spec(R)×P). Drinfeld's theorem makes the point-parameter map an isomorphism, so pointwise coefficient faithfulness cannot be a rational-top detector. The actual proper-curve product remains a different problem. These are the FF auxiliaries and the root receipt `ROOT_FIXED_FAMILY_TETRAHEDRAL_REVIEW.md`.

An explicit projective resolution of the tetrahedral hypersurface has been checked in every positive characteristic, including the special degree-five polynomial in characteristic five. Its four exceptional components are rational surfaces with15,10,5,0 point blowups, six double projective lines and four triple points. The complete36-case finite geometric certificate and its stated scope were inspected independently. This constructs a test model, not a resolution of the universal ordinary quotient.

On that exceptional divisor the full natural rational K-spectrum comparison gives33 copies of K(P) and one shift by−2. The raw punctured Chow group in codimension two is consequently K2(k) with rational coefficients: the four degree coordinates are removed by the entire exceptional-cycle image. The15-case matrix certificate was read in full, separately from the geometric and descent proofs. Ordinary algebraic equivalence still requires actual proper families, so this raw Chow computation is not a value of the ordinary quotient.

The universal Laurent-symbol family has a nonzero negative-K boundary by the Bass fundamental theorem although all its point specializations lift. Its finite p=2,3,5 polynomial certificate has34 passes and is explicitly restricted to polynomial identities. The exact source also does not assert arbitrary coherent essential surjectivity. For the higher positive-characteristic cokernel, the candidate deformed-K3 incidence construction has a support-divisor lifting mechanism for the indicated subgroup, rather than a counterexample. # Review 14 assessment: general component-restriction kernel removed

**Qualifying movement: YES. Consecutive dry reviews: 0.**

The prior review at 17:50 UTC counted C.3bm, C.3bn and C.3bo. Their endpoint comparison, degree elimination and component Albanese isogeny are not counted again here. The main positive-characteristic algebraizable normal strict-local threefold branch then had the exact S00(E) quotient, with both the component Albanese kernels and the entire kernel of restriction to the components still requiring actual proper-perfect-family relations.

C.3bt now proves, for the full negative SNC exceptional-surface scope of C.3bm, that the component map

`phi: F_gamma^2 K0(E)_Q -> direct_sum_i CH0(E_i)_Q`

is onto and its **entire kernel lies in J_K(E)**. There is no dual-complex H2, triple-point period or full component-to-edge Picard-restriction hypothesis. This removes a previously unresolved part of the general main branch, rather than restating the same quotient or proving only a restricted configuration. The exact remaining group is

`T_E / (phi(J_K(E) intersect S00(E)) + phi(Q intersect S00(E)))`,

where T_E is the direct sum of the smooth components' rational Albanese kernels and Q is the actual exceptional cycle image. The denominator splitting is proved using the common Poincare subgroup. The component Albanese-kernel quotient remains uncomputed. Vanishing when all those kernels vanish is a consequence of the general kernel-removal theorem, not an independent reason for this movement decision.

## Completed proof and certification

The marked-family lemma builds a single smooth connected proper parameter curve for each finite edge tuple. Normalized rational sections on the first parameter curve have finite divisor support; interpolation makes h vanish at every support point. On the connected cover `(g-1)^2 = h g`, every relevant residue of g is exactly one. Dennis–Stein extensions across its zeros and poles give a global K2 sheaf class; the natural Brown–Gersten/Postnikov filtration gives actual integral K1 families whose marked restrictions vanish on the entire proper parameter. The actual SNC totalization, negative degree-surjectivity identity and previously certified proper K2 symbol families remove all edge and vertex coherence ambiguity rationally. These are complete written proofs, including characteristic two and ramification, not finite-check inferences.

The complete VE.1–VE.10 and UK.1–UK.7 arguments passed root's independent proof/source audit at 18:41:37.559294 UTC, Frobenius's complete independent audit at 18:41:12 UTC, and Real Weil's complete marked-family and own-implication audit at 18:41–18:42 UTC. The authorship distinction is preserved. Root then reread both complete current texts, both peer receipts, the whole bounded program and every saved finite record. The owner's formal promotion completed at 18:48:51.902100 UTC after standalone compilation and actual inspection of all seven rendered pages. The final VE edit only changed its first status comment; the implication's two intervening repairs were typographic. Exact hashes, read scopes, runtime and QA are in the full receipts and `agents/cycles/output/C3bt_promotion.json`.

The 112 bounded checks and clean seven-page build support their stated computational and typesetting scopes. They are not counted as mathematical movement. The source audits distinguish full proof reads from statement-only reads and unsuccessful acquisitions; no priority is claimed.

# Review 15 assessment: no further main-gate reduction

**Qualifying movement: NO. Consecutive dry reviews: 1.**

Review14 counted C.3bt, which removed the entire component-restriction kernel in the general negative SNC exceptional-surface branch. That theorem is not counted again. The strongest remaining gate in this branch is still

`T_E / (phi(J_K(E) intersect S00(E)) + phi(Q intersect S00(E)))`,

where T_E is the direct sum of the rational Albanese kernels of the smooth exceptional components, J_K(E) consists of actual smooth-proper-curve endpoint relations in perfect K0, and Q is the actual exceptional-cycle image. This quotient has not been proved zero or nonzero in its unrestricted stated scope.

## New work and why it is not qualifying movement

C.3bu now proves that every prescribed top-gamma perfect-K0 class on the entire fixed exceptional product E×P lifts rationally to the fixed regular resolution product Z×P, under the exact algebraizable henselian positive-characteristic hypotheses of C.3bn. Finite perfect zero-scheme corrections lift integrally through regular surfaces, and they correct the whole p-power curve lift. In exceptional dimension at most two this yields full rational K0 surjectivity and K_-1(R×P)_Q=0. The complete proof passed root and both independent mathematical reviews; the final five-page owner build is clean and all five pages were actually inspected by the owner. This strengthens an auxiliary lifting theorem. It does not construct the globally compatible exceptional families required in the displayed quotient and does not reduce that remaining main gate.

The supported K1 construction, exact parameter-localization obstruction, regular-target specialization analysis, and relative elliptic countermodel delimit the valuation approach. They do not settle the missing symbolic-unit lift over R tensor_k k(u), a generally nonlocal base. The product-component argument proves further restricted cases; the blow-up calculation shows that a proposed divisor correction retains the original Picard obstruction. None proves a general vanishing or nonvanishing theorem for the displayed quotient.

The Kummer-fourfold incidence calculation proves even transcendental scalar for every integral Hodge surface cut in that particular incidence construction on a type-(1,2) abelian surface. Its lattice arithmetic, exact coefficient repair, and geometric normalization have been independently checked with explicit source scopes. It is a construction exclusion, not a nonalgebraicity theorem and not a classification of all integral correspondences. The proposed extension to higher Kummer dimensions is separately under examination and has not reduced the main existence gate. Source acquisition, additional proof audits, typesetting, and packaging do not count as mathematical movement.

## All remaining main gates retained

C.1/C.2 retain their universal standard-conjecture, positivity and arbitrary-source-image gates, including the first complex codimension-three fivefold issue. C.3 retains the global projective Griffiths/Beauville gates, rational top ordinary-B vanishing in general dimension at least three and arbitrary fields, the complex threefold case, the displayed positive-characteristic threefold quotient, and the higher positive-characteristic restriction ranges: injectivity for r≥2,N≥2r+1 and surjectivity for r≥2,N≥2r+2. The already settled ordinary complex restriction table is unchanged.

C.4 retains the integral End=Z type-(1,2) abelian-surface gate, the general nonprincipal threefold gate, and the supersingular threefold p=2,3 cases. D.4/E.2 retain the general motivic effectivity and common-polarization gates. A.8/Lead A3 retains the exact historical interpretation at15:70; E.5/Lead E3 retains the historical continuous-H1 interpretation issue. The following ledger table retains all41 Part and Lead rows and their exact recorded states.
